style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>触觉不是想加就能加的</span></strong><span>，论文把原因拆成了三层。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>第一层，数据太贵。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>现有的大规模机器人数据集主要面向平行夹爪——两根手指一捏一放，遥操作门槛低。但灵巧手有22个自由度，操作员要戴数据手套，每一个手势都要精确映射到机械手上，还要保证视觉、触觉、关节状态严格对齐，目前灵巧手遥操作成本是平行夹爪的5到10倍。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>第二层，频率对不上。视觉模型处理一帧图像动辄几百毫秒，只能跑到5到10Hz。但触觉反应需要毫秒级，20Hz以上才能捕捉到“正在滑”和“已经滑了”的临界点。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>两种信号塞进同一个低频Transformer，视觉来不及看，触觉来不及反应。</span></p><section style=\"text-align: center;\"><img src=\"http://cdn.linkresearcher.com/dpjsnkt1-nhyz-n4bi-x3fn-uchwod5y-medium\"></section><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>第三层，表征太浅。很多方法把触觉当成一个静态的数字——“当前压力5牛顿”。但触觉本质是时序信号：滑动、插入、按压、搓动，每种接触状态都有力随时间变化的独特模式。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>好，问题在这里，先看看T-Rex团队解决的怎么样。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>在12项真实世界的灵巧操作任务上，T-Rex平均成功率达到65%，比最强纯视觉基线的35%高出了30个百分点。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>翻书页96%对68%，开锁70%对0%——纯视觉方案在这个任务上完全被碾压。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>消融实验更直接：把T-Rex的触觉输入全部拿掉，成功率从65%掉到42%，降了23个百分点——12项任务里超过三分之一的有效操作纯靠“手感”撑着。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>数据效率也很惊人：仅给10条微调演示时，经过中期训练的T-Rex能达到约40%成功率，没经过中期训练的模型直接归零。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>这说明模型不是“背下了”数据，而是真正理解了“搓”“捏”“拧”这些动作的通用手感。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>一句话总结：视觉数据对灵巧手来说，本质上是不够用的——触觉不是锦上添花，而是必选项。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>谁先解决“触觉怎么加”的架构问题，谁就可能在灵巧手这个赛道上卡住位置</span></strong><span>。</span></p><h2 style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-family: Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif;font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;margin: 40px 0px;line-height: 1.5;font-weight: bold;padding-left: 15px;word-break: break-all !important;border-left: 6px solid rgb(0, 153, 127) !important;font-size: 20px !important;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;\"><span>怎么做到的？</span></h2><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>T-Rex的核心思路并不复杂——给触觉单独开一条高速通路，而不是让它和视觉挤在同一条慢车道上。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>整体架构叫Mixture-of-Transformer-Experts，三个专家分工明确。</span></p><section style=\"text-align: center;\"><img src=\"http://cdn.linkresearcher.com/w9ph8nz1-atns-i3vr-b42v-fk9v6crx-medium\"></section><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>Latent Expert处理视觉和语言，预测未来的视觉表征，相当于给模型提供一个“接下来会发生什么”的大局感。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>Action Expert做低频动作规划，参数量1.41B，是三个专家里最大的那个，跑一次管一个动作块。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>Tactile Expert做高频触觉精修，参数量只有0.62B，轻量到可以频繁触发。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>关键机制是Cascaded Flow Matching：</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>扩散去噪通常需要10步才能从一团随机噪声变成一个干净的动作轨迹，而T-Rex在第4步处一切两半：</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>前6步由Action Expert完成，生成一个“大方向对了但缺乏手感细节”的半成品；后4步由Tactile Expert接手，注入实时触觉数据完成精修。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>为什么是第4步？作者团队通过实验对比发现太早切，Action Expert去噪步数太少，继承不了大规模人类视频预训练获得的先验知识；太晚切，动作分布已经定型，触觉信号进来也来不及修正了。</span></p><section style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><img alt=\"飞书文档 - 图片\" src=\"http://cdn.linkresearcher.com/2g3jzoet-e4h1-sqvr-9wez-2p8ay619-medium\"></section><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>第4步刚好处在“该有的形状都有了，但细节还能改”的黄金位置。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>这就像写文章：主编（Action Expert）定好大框架后就去忙别的了，四个编辑（Tactile Expert）在不同的节点上轮番检查细节，不需要主编每次都回来重看一遍全文。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>触觉信号怎么编码是另一个关键设计。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>触觉传感器有零点漂移和高频噪声，直接拿原始电压值喂给模型，模型很可能把传感器噪声当成接触特征学进去。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>T-Rex用VQ-VAE把过去16帧（约0.5秒）的力历史压缩成64个离散码字。不同接触状态——按压、插入、滑动——会被自动聚类到不同的码字上。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>这样一来，传感器漂移被过滤掉了，触觉信号还变得可解释——你能看到模型“理解”了什么类型的接触。</span></p><section style=\"text-align: center;\"><img src=\"http://cdn.linkresearcher.com/8one2f70-j25f-67ne-9pnw-73bvyjru-medium\"></section><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>两个工程细节，第一个：让码本“活起来”。 VQ-VAE码本，理训练时经常出现“码本坍塌”——大部分抽屉空着，少数几个塞满各种杂乱状态。T-Rex的做法是定期检查哪些抽屉闲置，主动塞点数据进去“激活”它，确保所有码字都被利用起来。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>第二个，给“有手感”的时刻更高权重。 避免模型化90%的精力去学好“零接触”这个最容易学的状态，而忽略真正有价值的接触瞬间。T-Rex给高力帧更高的学习权重，让模型把算力集中在关键接触点上。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>论文正文不会写、但做灵巧手工程化的人一看就懂。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>最后是训练策略。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>T-Rex采用三阶段训练：</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>先在大规模人类视频上预训练（22,889小时），让模型看懂“人是怎么动的”；</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>再用100小时遥操作数据做中期训练，覆盖207种物体和22种动作基元——目的是把人类视频里的运动知识迁移到机器人本体上，而不是死磕某个具体任务。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>最后用约100条任务演示做后训练，快速适配特定需求。</span></p><section style=\"text-align: center;\"><img src=\"http://cdn.linkresearcher.com/znv0w5y9-g23r-bzki-3n2x-vh7tcmp6-medium\"></section><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>其中最核心是中期训练，解决了一个根本问题：人手抓杯子的方式和机械手抓杯子的方式不一样，策略不一样，受力反馈也不一样。如果直接拿人类视频训练出来的模型去操控机器人，它不知道怎么把“看来的”转化成“做出来的”。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>中期训练就是用100小时的遥操作数据，把这道鸿沟填上。它不是让模型死磕某一个具体任务，而是让模型接触207种物体和22种动作基元的组合，理解“搓”这个动作在不同物体上应该怎么执行、“捏”这个动作需要多大的力。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>这就像一个人学完游泳理论后，在浅水区把蛙泳、自由泳、仰泳都练了一遍——不追求速度，只追求“会用”。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>有了这个基础，最后阶段只用100条任务演示就能快速适配——因为模型已经知道“手感”是什么了，只需要知道“用在哪儿”。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>T-Rex的技术路线清晰指向一个判断——视觉和触觉在灵巧操作中不是主从关系，而是并行关系。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>它用一个异步架构解决了频率不匹配的底层矛盾，用一套时序编码把漂移的传感器信号变成了可解释的离散词汇，用一组基元组合的覆盖策略替代了特定任务的深度堆叠。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>这三件事单看都不是“发明新数学”，但组合在一起，为灵巧手的触觉利用提供了一套可复用的系统方案。</span></p><h2 style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-family: Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif;font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;margin: 40px 0px;line-height: 1.5;font-weight: bold;padding-left: 15px;word-break: break-all !important;border-left: 6px solid rgb(0, 153, 127) !important;font-size: 20px !important;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;\"><span>灵巧手，存在一个“第一性原理”吗？</span></h2><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>灵巧手赛道眼下最热闹的争论，集中在两个地方：关节运动形式和自由度数量。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>腱绳还是连杆？丝杠还是齿轮？11个自由度够用还是得干到27个？不同厂商各执一词，技术路线远未收敛。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>这些争论当然都有意义——传动方案决定成本、可靠性和使用寿命，自由度决定灵巧程度的上限——但学术前沿的核心关注，不在这些问题中的任何一个上面。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>因为一个更深层的问题很少被讨论：有了这些硬件，算法能不能用好？</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>传统大模型范式几乎以视觉数据为主，没有针对触觉模态的有效利用方案。T-Rex论文里那个17%掉到6%的实验，恰好说明了这一点——不是触觉没用，是现有多模态大模型架构消化不了触觉。</span></p><section style=\"text-align: center;\"><img src=\"http://cdn.linkresearcher.com/nceqhbi3-2om3-e7a8-r8at-i7w8fogb-medium\"></section><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>T-Rex团队的探索最大价值可能在这里： 跳出硬件参数的争论，回到一个更根本的问题——灵巧手做精细操作，到底需要什么数据？</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>关节形式和自由度数量解决的是“能不能动”的问题，触觉数据解决的是“能不能感知和反应”的问题，由此衍生出需要什么样的触觉数据这个体系化问题，并给出了一个能泛化的方案。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>当然，T-Rex这项研究得以完成，一个关键前提是Sharpa Wave提供的硬件基础——22个自由度、180Hz高频触觉信号、稳定的传感器输出。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>灵巧手的硬件天花板顶高了，T-Rex才得以在算法层面去够这个上限。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>反过来，T-Rex的算法探索也给硬件提出了新要求：手掌区域需要触觉感知，不同传感器的数据格式需要统一。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>T-Rex的软件、SHARPA的硬件探索方向，后续可能会给这个细分领域带来更深刻的变化。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>首先灵巧手可能从具身智能本体中分化出来，成为一个独立赛道——占整机成本15%到20%，经济上可行，技术上门槛也足够高。</span></p><section><img src=\"http://cdn.linkresearcher.com/6bsmf9gy-2xf1-z4ap-bagh-85zw0ick-medium\"></section><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>但如果触觉数据成为核心壁垒，灵巧手就不仅仅是执行器，而是整个感知-决策-执行链条中数据价值最高的一环。谁掌握触觉数据的采集能力和模型训练能力，谁就掌握了议价权。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>第二个变化，更多专用场景的具身应用可能被启发。当前具身智能的主流叙事是“通用”——一个模型解决所有任务。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>但这个叙事在接触密集型任务上遇到了困难，因为通用数据里触觉是缺失的。T-Rex则证明的是另一种可能性：在一个足够具体的垂直场景里，用专用的数据模态（触觉）、专用的架构设计（异步级联）、专用的数据收集策略（基元×物体），可以取得比“通用”方案好得多的效果。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>这条路径如果走通，会启发更多人去寻找类似的高价值专用场景。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>T-Rex的贡献不是发明了“新数学”，而是把现有工具组合成一套可用的系统方案，试图建立具身智能触觉数据统一的采集、训练范式，推动灵巧手真正scale up起来。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span><span style=\"font-weight: bold;font-style: italic;\">项目地址：</span></span><span><span style=\"font-weight: bold;font-style: italic;\">https://tactile-reactive-dexterous.github.io/</span></span></p><h2 style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-family: Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif;font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;margin: 40px 0px;line-height: 1.5;font-weight: bold;padding-left: 15px;word-break: break-all !important;border-left: 6px solid rgb(0, 153, 127) !important;font-size: 20px !important;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;\"><span>作者简介</span></h2><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>牛丹彤</span></strong><span>，UC Berkeley PhD Candidate，NVIDIA Gear Lab实习生，导师Trevor Darrell，主要从事具身灵巧手大模型的研究，曾以第一作者或共同第一作者身份在CVPR, ICML, ICLR等国际顶级会议上发表论文8篇，领导或主要贡献T-Rex，EgoScale等工作，目前谷歌学术引用1100+。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>刘卓洋</span></strong><span>，北京大学元培学院本科生，导师仉尚航，目前在UC Berkeley访问，导师Trevor Darrell，主要从事具身多模态推理大模型方向的研究，曾以第一作者或共同第一作者身份在ICML, ICLR, NeurIPS, ICRA等国际顶级会议上发表论文6篇，其他参与论文共计15篇，目前谷歌学术引用400余次。曾获2026商汤奖学金（全国30人），2025北京大学学术新星提名，北京大学元培学院年度科研奖励，北京大学新生奖学金等。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>Trevor Darrell</span></strong><span>教授，最广为人知的成果是开发了Caffe深度学习库。这个库在深度学习领域具有重要的地位，为众多的研究人员和开发者提供了便捷的工具，极大地推动了深度学习技术的发展。</span></p><p style=\"color: rgb(34, 34, 34);font-style: normal;font-variant-ligatures: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: normal;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-size: 16px;font-family: Arial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>Trevor Darrell现在是加州大学伯克利分校Berkeley DeepDrive研究中心的主任。同时担任伯克利EECS系计算机科学分部的教职，并受聘于加州大学附属的国际计算机科学研究所（ICSI）。研究方向涵盖大规模感知学习算法，包括物体与行为识别及检测，并应用于机器人及移动设备的多模态交互等多种场景。他的研究兴趣包括计算机视觉、机器学习、计算机图形学以及基于感知的人机交互界面。</span></p><section style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;\"><section><section><section><section style=\"margin-top: 10px;margin-bottom: 10px;letter-spacing: 0.578px;color: rgb(62, 62, 62);font-size: 16px;text-align: style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（</span></span><span style=\"font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang normal;\">愿景的核心。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">然而，一直以来，研究</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">遗传</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（基因）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">和</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">化学</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（药物）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">的实验室往往是两条平行线。遗传筛选通量高，但难以直接成药，化学筛选针对治疗却因化学空间浩瀚无比而效率低下。如果能用一个统一的 AI 模型桥接这两大领域，打破遗传与化学扰动之间的壁垒，不仅能揭示更深层的生物学机制，还能极大加速药物研发。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></p><section style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（AI for Life Sciences Lab）</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span bold;\">Cell</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\"> 上发表了</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">题为：</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);\">UniPert-G2CP bridges genetic and chemical screens from molecular representation to phenotype modeling</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\"> </span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">的研究论文。</span></span></span></span></section><section style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;\"><span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></span></span></section><section style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;\"><span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">该研究构建了一个名为 </span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(217, 33, 66);font-weight: bold;\">UniPert-G2CP</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\"> 的全新 AI 框架，通过整合多模态分子扰动因子</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（原因）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">表征，并实现从遗传到化学扰动表型</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（结果）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">的迁移学习，从而连接遗传筛选与化学筛选，从而更准确地预测扰动、发现作用机制，并实现高效的计算机辅助药物筛选。</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(217, 33, 66);font-weight: bold;\">UniPert-G2CP</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(217, 33, 66);\"> 推动了通用生物学因果建模的发展，加速了</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(217, 33, 66);font-weight: bold;\">人工智能虚拟细胞</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);font-weight: normal;\">（AIVC）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(217, 33, 66);\">的实现，并拓展了 AI 驱动的精准医学的应用潜力。</span></span></span></span></section><section style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, bold;\">化学</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">扰动因子）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">的表型效应，已成为</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: 136);\">（</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">Artificial Intelligence Virtual Cell，</span></span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft
