style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\"> </span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">Connectivity Map、Cancer Dependency Map </span></span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">及 </span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">Cell Painting Gallery </span></span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">等项目所提供的具有上下文特异性的、高通量、多领域的扰动后表型特征数据。通过对这些扰动因子所引起的细胞响应与行为进行联合建模与分析，可系统性地探索生物学机制和治疗干预手段，同时有助于识别潜在的治疗靶点，并发现针对特定疾病的新型药物候选物，从而推动高效的数据驱动型个体化医疗。然而，这一前景广阔的范式在</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">细胞扰动建模</span></span><span style=\"font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">方面仍面临若干关键瓶颈——</span></span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-bottom: 16px;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">1、模态差异大</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">： 基因是由 A、G、C、T 组成的长链序列，而药物通常是原子构成的小分子化合物，传统的 AI 模型很难将两者映射到同一个语义空间中。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-bottom: 16px;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">2、数据规模不对等</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">： 基因库相对有限</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（人类只有大约 2 万个基因）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">，可以通过 CRISPR 技术进行大规模并行筛选；而化学药物的空间近乎无限</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（估计有</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">超过 10</span></span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);font-weight: normal;\">⁶³</span></span><span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\"> 种化合物</span></span></span></span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">，实验筛选成本高昂，覆盖率极低。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">3、细胞异质性</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">： 同样的药，对不同来源的细胞</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（例如不同患者的癌细胞）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">效果可能截然不同，模型需要具备跨环境的泛化能力。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;text-align: center;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(2, 30, 170);font-weight: bold;\">破局：UniPert-G2CP 是如何工作的？</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;text-align: justify;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">为了攻克上述难题，研究团队设计了一个</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">两阶段深度学习框架</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">，以克服跨扰动域、分子模态和文库规模的表型筛选中的建模瓶颈。在第一阶段，研究团队开发了 </span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(217, 33, 66);font-weight: bold;\">UniPert</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);text-decoration: underline;\">Uni</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">fied Multimodal </span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);text-decoration: underline;\">Pert</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">urbagen Representation Learning）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">，这是一个基于对比学习的多模态分子表征学习模型，能够将大分子和小分子编码到共享的语义嵌入空间中，从而桥接遗传扰动与化学扰动。在第二阶段，研究团队将 UniPert 集成到一个扰动效应预测模型中，开发出了 </span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(217, 33, 66);font-weight: bold;\">G2CP</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);text-decoration: underline;\">G</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">enetic-</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);text-decoration: underline;\">to</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">-</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);text-decoration: underline;\">C</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">hemical </span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);text-decoration: underline;\">P</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">erturbation Transfer Learning）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">，这是一种遗传到化学扰动的迁移学习方法，利用系统性的基于 CRISPR 的遗传筛选来提升计算药物筛选的效率。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;text-align: justify;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"text-align: center;line-height: 1.5em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span><img src=\"http://cdn.linkresearcher.com/uk290s7r-9dyr-dwv2-jqmw-q9l8hweg-medium\" style=\"box-shadow: rgb(240, 240, 240) 0px 0px 0.5em 0px;background-color: transparent;width: 100%;\"><img></span><span style=\"font-size: 13px;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">UniPert-G2CP 概述</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">第一阶段：UniPert——打造通用的分子“语言”</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-bottom: 16px;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">UniPert</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\"> 的核心任务是将基因</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（编码的蛋白质）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">和小分子药物转化为同一种“AI 语言”</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（向量嵌入）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-bottom: 16px;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">对于药物</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（小分子）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">： 它使用了经典的 ECFP 指纹作为输入，这相当于提取了药物的结构骨架特征。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-bottom: 16px;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">对于基因</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（编码的蛋白质）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">： 这是创新的关键，它不仅仅依赖最新的蛋白质语言模型</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（例如 ESM）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">来获取全局特征，还结合了多序列比对（MSA）和图神经网络</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（GNN）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">。这就像一个既懂宏观语法又精通局部方言的专家，能更好地捕捉蛋白质的保守功能区域。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">对齐训练： 利用已知的化合物-靶点相互作用</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（CPI）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">数据，通过对比学习，强制让相互作用的药物和蛋白在向量空间中“贴在一起”。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">结果显示，UniPert 不仅学会了区分不同作用机制的药物，甚至能识别出结合在同一蛋白不同位点的结构不相似的药物</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（例如作用于 GABAA 受体的不同变构调节剂）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">。对于蛋白来说，它能准确地将功能相似的蛋白家族聚集在一起，甚至能推断出未被充分研究的蛋白所属家族或相互作用关系。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">第二阶段：G2CP——“以基因为师，导药入海”</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-bottom: 16px;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">有了 </span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">UniPert </span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">这个强大的“翻译器”，</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">G2CP</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\"> 框架就能大显身手了，它的策略是“遗传预训练 + 化学微调”。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-bottom: 16px;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">预训练</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（学基因）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">： 首先，利用大量、系统性的 CRISPR 基因筛选数据来训练模型。这让模型深刻理解了“敲除某个基因会导致细胞发生什么变化”这一底层逻辑，给模型打下了坚实的生物学基础。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">微调</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（学药物）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">： 由于化学药物数据少，直接用少量药物数据训练效果差。G2CP 的做法是将 UniPert 编码的药物信息输入模型，利用从基因中学到的知识，去预测未见过的药物会引起的细胞反应。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">这就像先在题库</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（基因库）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">里做了海量练习，掌握了解题规律，然后再参加考试，遇到新题型</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（药物）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">，也能举一反三，准确作答。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;text-align: center;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(2, 30, 170);font-weight: bold;\">成绩：效果显著，洞察深刻</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">论文中的实验结果令人印象深刻——</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">1、预测更准确</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">： 在多个数据集</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（例如 LINCS、sciPlex3）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">上，G2CP 预测药物诱导的基因表达变化</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（转录组）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">和形态学变化的准确度远超现有方法。特别是在数据稀缺的情况下，性能提升尤为明显</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（高达 375%）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">2、搞定“难搞”的药物</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">： 对于组蛋白去乙酰化酶</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（HDAC）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">抑制剂这类结构相似但药效有细微差别的药物，UniPert-G2CP 也能精准区分它们的分子特征和预期效果。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">3、揭示耐药机制</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">： 作为一个案例研究，研究团队聚焦于乳腺癌中的雌激素受体</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（ER）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">，成功复现了 ER 突变</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（例如 Y537S、D538G）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">如何导致对现有药物</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（例如氟维司群）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">产生耐药性，并指出了潜在的耐药相关基因通路</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（例如 MYC、DNA 修复通路）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">。这为克服内分泌治疗耐药提供了新的计算视角。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;text-align: center;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(2, 30, 170);font-weight: bold;\">意义与展望：迈向虚拟细胞时代</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;text-align: center;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-bottom: 16px;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span><span><span><span><span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">UniPert-G2CP</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\"> 的</span></span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">核心是一个通用计算框架，通过统一因果和效应两个视角下的扰动表征，结合多模态分子表征学习和跨域扰动表型迁移学习，显著提升了生物学因果模型在不同数据模态、实验尺度和生物学背景下的泛化能力。该研究的意义不</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">止于开发了一个新算法，更为整个领域带来了新范式——</span></span></span></span></span></span></p><ul class=\"\" style=\"list-style-type: disc;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><li><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-bottom: 16px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">加速药物发现</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">： 通过“遗传指导化学”，可以用计算机模拟海量化合物的效果，优先筛选出最有潜力的候选药物，大幅降低湿实验的成本和时间。</span></span></p></li><li><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-bottom: 16px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">赋能精准医疗</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">： 模型能考虑到不同细胞系</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（患者来源）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">的异质性，未来有望根据患者的基因背景，预测其个性化用药反应，实现真正的“对症下药”。</span></span></p></li><li><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">奠定 AIVC 基础</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">： UniPert-G2CP 提供了一种统一建模“原因”</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（扰动因子）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">和“结果”</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（细胞表型）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">的通用框架，是推动构建全功能</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">人工智能虚拟细胞</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（AIVC）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">的重要一步。</span></span></p></li></ul><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span><span><span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><br></span></span></span></span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-size: 15px;letter-spacing: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">总的来说，</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">UniPert-G2CP</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\"> 的出现，标志着我们在利用 AI 解析复杂生命系统、连接基因型与表型方面迈出了坚实一步。它不仅是一个强大的预测工具，更是一个生成假设的平台，有望在未来帮助我们更快地发现新药、破解疾病机制，最终造福人类健康。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft from prestigious universities and large research groups are significantly more likely to have their research published in general-interest, top-tier scientific journals. Meanwhile, authors based in China and those studying politics, economics, gender or other social issues face a significant disadvantage.</p><p>来自知名大学和大型研究团体的科学家，其研究成果在综合性顶级科学期刊上发表的可能性要大得多。与此同时，以来自中国的作者，以及研究政治、经济、性别或其他社会问题的作者，则面临着显著的劣势。</p><br><p>These are a few takeaways from a sweeping new University of Colorado Boulder-led study, published July 15 in the journal &#x3C;i>Science Advances&#x3C;/i>.</p><p>美国科罗拉多大学博尔德分校（University of Colorado Boulder）领衔的一项广泛新研究于7月15日发表在《科学进展》（&#x3C;i>Science Advances&#x3C;/i>）期刊上，以下是该研究的几点重要发现。</p><br><p>The analysis of more than 110,000 manuscript submissions over five years offers the most comprehensive glimpse yet at who makes the cut and who doesn't during the rigorous editorial and peer-review process at elite science journals.</p><p>一项历时五年、涵盖逾11万份稿件提交的分析，为我们提供了迄今为止最全面的视角，揭示了在精英科学期刊严苛的编辑与同行评审过程中，究竟谁能脱颖而出，谁又遗憾止步。</p><br><p>\"These journals play a special role in the science ecosystem, shaping what ideas get attention, inform society and shape policy,\" said senior author Aaron Clauset, a professor of computer science and researcher with CU Boulder's BioFrontiers Institute. \"Yet very few studies have been able to open the black box and take a look at their review process.\"</p><p>资深作者、计算机科学教授兼科罗拉多大学博尔德分校生物前沿研究所研究员Aaron Clauset指出：“这些期刊在科学生态系统中发挥着特殊作用，它们影响着哪些观点获得关注、如何为社会提供信息并塑造政策走向。然而，目前极少有研究能够揭开其评审过程的神秘面纱，深入探究其运作机制。”</p><br><p>The findings raise an important question: To what degree are important scientific discoveries going unnoticed because their authors lack certain characteristics?</p><p>这些发现提出了一个重要问题：有多少重要的科学发现因为其作者缺乏某些特质而被忽视？</p><br><p>By calling out potential biases and making their data set available to the public, the authors also hope to help journals improve their processes.</p><p>通过指出潜在偏见并向公众开放数据集，作者们还希望帮助期刊改进其审稿流程。</p><br><p>\"With the rise of AI slop and the ability to mass-produce junk science, the role of established gatekeepers like these elite journals becomes more important,\" said first author Sam Zhang, an assistant professor of mathematics and statistics at the University of Vermont who earned his doctorate in applied math from CU Boulder.</p><p>“随着人工智能垃圾内容的泛滥以及批量生产伪科学能力的提升，这些精英期刊作为权威把关者的角色变得愈发重要，”该研究第一作者、佛蒙特大学数学与统计学助理教授Sam Zhang表示。他此前在科罗拉多大学博尔德分校获得应用数学博士学位。</p><br><p>&#x3C;font style=\"font-size: 18px; color: rgb(28, 72, 127);\">&#x3C;b>Probing the 'science of science'&#x3C;/b>&#x3C;/font></p><p>&#x3C;font style=\"font-size: 18px; color: rgb(28, 72, 127);\">&#x3C;b>探究科学背后的科学&#x3C;/b>&#x3C;/font></p><br><p>For a decade, Clauset has studied what he calls \"the science of science,\" scouring data to paint a clear picture of who enters (and leaves) academia, who gets tenure and whose ideas tend to spread.</p><p>十年来，Clauset一直致力于他所称的“科学学”研究，通过梳理大量数据，试图清晰描绘出学术界的人员流动图景——哪些人进入（或离开）学术圈，哪些人获得终身教职，以及哪些思想观点更易传播。</p><br><p>\"There is no science without scientists, so it's important to know who has the opportunity to be 'in the room,' so to speak, and who does not, to make discoveries,\" said Clauset.</p><p>Clauset表示：“没有科学家就没有科学，因此重要的是要知道，在某种意义上，谁有机会'登堂入室'去发现，而谁没有。”</p><br><p>For the new study, Clauset and Zhang were joined by Dan Larremore, associate professor of computer science at CU, and Nick Laberge, then a doctoral candidate there. They analyzed anonymized manuscript records for 110,303 submissions to &#x3C;i>Science&#x3C;/i> and Science Advances between 2016 and 2020.</p><p>在这项新研究中，Clauset和Zhang与科罗拉多大学计算机科学副教授Dan Larremore以及当时的博士生Nick Laberge合作。他们分析了2016年至2020年间提交给《科学》（&#x3C;i>Science&#x3C;/i>）及《科学进展》期刊的11万零303份投稿记录的匿名数据。</p><br><p>Unlike topical journals, general-interest journals cover everything from health to space to politics and are regularly covered by mainstream media. They strongly influence careers, with grants and tenure often tied to publication in them.</p><p>与专业期刊不同，综合类期刊涵盖从健康到太空再到政治等广泛领域，并经常受到主流媒体的报道。它们对科研人员的职业生涯有着重大影响，因为研究经费和终身教职往往与在这些期刊上发表文章紧密相关。</p><br><p>The competition is stiff. In 2023 alone, Science received more than 15,000 submissions.</p><p>竞争异常激烈。仅2023年，《科学》杂志就收到了超过15000份投稿。</p><br><p>As a result, such journals have a two-tiered review process: In-house editors make a first pass and then enlist outside subject experts to make recommendations before accepting or rejecting manuscripts.</p><p>因此，这类期刊采用双重评审流程：首先由内部编辑进行初审，随后在决定接受或拒绝稿件前，会邀请外部学科专家提供评审意见。</p><br><p>Over five years, the study found, Science accepted just 6.1% of submissions; Science Advances accepted 10.5%. About 83% of submissions to Science and nearly 75% to Science Advances were screened and rejected by in-house editors before undergoing peer review. It also found that:</p><p>研究发现，在过去的五年里，《科学》杂志仅接受了6.1%的投稿；《科学进展》的接受率为10.5%。大约83%投向《科学》杂志的稿件以及近75%投向《科学进展》的稿件，在进入同行评审之前，就已被内部编辑筛选并拒绝。研究还发现：</p><br><p>- Papers with 10 or more authors are three times as likely to be published as papers with 1–5 authors.</p><p>- 作者数量为10人或以上的论文被发表的可能性是作者数量为1至5人的论文的三倍。</p><br><p>- Manuscripts with corresponding authors from the most prestigious institutions had an 11.6% acceptance rate, compared with 3.4% for those from the least prestigious institutions.</p><p>- 来自最知名机构的投稿，其通讯作者的文章接受率为11.6%，而来自最不知名机构的投稿接受率仅为3.4%。</p><br><p>- Studies with authors from China were less than one-third as likely to be published as papers with U.S.- or Canada-based authors. Notably, authors with Chinese names but based in the U.S. also faced a disadvantage.</p><p>- 与中国作者相关的研究论文发表概率，比基于美国或加拿大作者的研究低至不足三分之一。值得注意的是，拥有中国姓名但身在美国的作者同样面临这一劣势。</p><br><p>- Even before COVID, research related to viruses, infection, RNA and immune response had a sixfold greater likelihood of being accepted than research on politics, economics, gender and the societal dimensions of health.</p><p>- 早在新冠疫情之前，关于病毒、感染、RNA和免疫反应的研究，其被采纳的可能性就已比涉及政治、经济、性别及健康社会维度等领域的研究高出六倍。</p><br><p>- Work by female corresponding authors was slightly less likely to be accepted. Had this trend not existed, Science would have published about 23 more papers by women per year.</p><p>- 女性通讯作者提交的研究成果被接受的可能性略低。若不存在这一趋势，《科学》杂志每年将多发表约23篇由女性撰写的论文。</p><br><p>\"The fact that we find only a very marginal association with gender is in some sense reassuring,\" said Clauset. \"But the number is still not zero.\"</p><p>“我们发现性别与犯罪之间的关联微乎其微，这在某种意义上令人宽慰，”Clauset说，“但这个数字仍然不是零。”</p><br><p>&#x3C;font style=\"font-size: 18px; color: rgb(28, 72, 127);\">&#x3C;b>Are editors and reviewers biased?&#x3C;/b>&#x3C;/font></p><p>&#x3C;font style=\"font-size: 18px; color: rgb(28, 72, 127);\">&#x3C;b>编辑和审稿人是否存在偏见？&#x3C;/b>&#x3C;/font></p><br><p>The authors stress that the findings do not prove that journal editors or reviewers are inherently biased. Instead, they shine a light on questions they and other researchers should explore.</p><p>作者强调，这些发现并不能证明期刊编辑或审稿人天生带有偏见。相反，它们揭示了他们和其他研究人员应该探索的问题。</p><br><p>For instance, Clauset notes, the prestige association was evident throughout the editorial and peer-review processes. Are reviewers inherently biased toward papers that come from Harvard or Stanford, or are those papers truly of better quality?</p><p>Clauset指出，在整个编辑和同行评审过程中，声望关联现象显而易见。评审人是否天生就对来自哈佛或斯坦福的论文抱有偏见？还是这些论文的质量确实更高？</p><br><p>Are journals somehow biased against China-based researchers, or do Chinese government incentives and other factors prompt China-based authors to submit an inordinately high number of papers?</p><p>期刊是否对中国研究人员存在某种偏见，还是中国政府的激励措施及其他因素促使中国作者提交了数量异常庞大的论文？</p><br><p>In the spirit of transparency, the American Association for the Advancement of Science (the journals' parent organization) volunteered to share the data, as long as the team developed a way to anonymize it.</p><p>本着透明的精神，美国科学促进会（这些期刊的上级组织）自愿分享数据，前提是研究团队需制定数据匿名化的方案。</p><br><p>\"The current evidence suggests, overall, these journals seem to be doing pretty well at just treating the science as the science. But this is not a totally clean bill of health,\" said Clauset. \"In the current environment where the integrity of science is being attacked in bad faith, we need to be thinking about whether the processes at these journals live up to the ideals the scientific community has set for them.\"</p><p>“目前的证据总体表明，这些期刊在将科学视为科学本身方面做得相当不错。但这并非完全的健康证明，”Clauset指出。“在当前科学诚信受到恶意攻击的环境下，我们需要思考这些期刊的运作流程是否达到了科学界为其设定的理想标准。”</p>,Scientists from prestigious universities and large research groups are significantly more likely to have their research published in general…,https://www.linkresearcher.com/theses/9c7dd4c1-ccaf-42cd-98cc-e0c5864b359c,Sam rgb(136, 136, 136);\"><span>如何才能让更多的优质内容以更短路径到达读者群体，缩短读者寻找优质内容的成本呢？</span><strong><span>答案就是：你不认识的人。</span></strong></span></p><p style=\"margin-right: 0em;margin-left: 0em;\"><span><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-right: 0em;margin-left: 0em;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\"><span>总有一些你不认识的人，知道你想知道的东西。PaperWeekly 或许可以成为一座桥梁，促使不同背景、不同方向的学者和学术灵感相互碰撞，迸发出更多的可能性。 </span></span></p><p style=\"margin-right: 0em;margin-left: 0em;\"><span><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-right: 0em;margin-left: 0em;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\"><span>PaperWeekly   </span></span><span><span style=\"font-size: 15px;\">国防科技大学的研究团队发表了一篇关于基于神经辐射场（NeRF）和3D高斯溅射（3DGS）的三维编辑技术的综合综述。系统地将现有方法归类为外观编辑、对象变换、形状变形、场景修复和创意编辑五大任务，深入分析了当前面临的多视角一致性、计算效率等核心挑战，为个性化三维内容创作领域的未来研究提供了重要参考。</span></span></p><p style=\"text-align: center;\"><span><img src=\"http://cdn.linkresearcher.com/rzmf3exu-gday-n0wp-jbfe-ymj83onc-medium\"></span></p><p><b><span><br></span></b></p></section><section style=\"text-align: viewBox=\"0 0 1 1\"></svg></section></section></section></section></section></section><p style=\"line-height: 2em;\"><span>   </span><span style=\"max-width: 100%;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-variant-ligatures: none;font-variant-caps: normal;letter-spacing: 0.578px;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;word-spacing: 0px;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: break-spaces;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-style: normal;font-weight: 400;text-align: justify;font-size: 16px;color: rgb(62, 62, 62);box-sizing: border-box;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;\"><span style=\"font-size: 15px;\">   传统的三维编辑需对网格和纹理进行手动调整，过程较为复杂。相比之下，基于辐射场的方法允许更直接地操作三维场景，用户可通过文本或交互指令修改外观与几何形状。该综述系统梳理了这一领域的发展脉络，呈现了NeRF与3DGS在影视、虚拟现实（VR）及增强现实（AR）等数字内容创作中的潜在影响。</span></span></p><p><span><span style=\"font-size: 15px;\">       </span><span style=\"font-size: 15px;color: rgb(33, 78, 160);font-weight: bold;\">应对个性化三维内容需求：开启灵活创作的新时代</span></span></p><p><span><span style=\"font-size: 15px;\">      随着三维内容个性化需求的提升，高效编辑三维资产的重要性日益凸显。传统显式建模方法在计算效率与细节保留之间难以兼顾。</span></span></p><p><span><span style=\"font-size: 15px;\">      NeRF和3DGS融合了高效的三维表示与高质量渲染，为三维编辑提供了新的技术路径。这些方法不仅推动了三维重建的发展，也支持从风格迁移到几何变形等多样化编辑操作，拓展了传统建模流程的可行性边界。</span></span></p><p><span><span style=\"font-size: 15px;\">     </span><span style=\"font-size: 15px;color: rgb(33, 78, 160);\"> </span><span style=\"font-size: 15px;color: rgb(33, 78, 160);font-weight: bold;\">综述关键发现：五大任务分类与解析</span></span></p><p><span><span style=\"font-size: 15px;\">      将现有研究系统归类为五大任务：外观编辑、对象变换、形状变形、场景修复与创意编辑。研究指出，技术发展呈现从早期CLIP方法向扩散模型演进的趋势，其中NeRF在渲染质量上表现良好，而3DGS在实时交互方面更具潜力。当前仍面临编辑效率与视觉质量的平衡、多视角一致性保持，以及大规模场景处理等挑战。</span></span></p><p><span><span style=\"font-size: 15px;\">      </span><span style=\"font-size: 15px;color: rgb(33, 78, 160);font-weight: bold;\">技术解密：多样化手段实现精准操控</span></span></p><p><span><span style=\"font-size: 15px;\">      本文详细剖析了不同技术路线的运作机制。外观编辑方法利用基于调色板的分解或风格迁移技术来改变视觉美感，同时保持照片级真实感。形状变形方法利用基于笼子（Cage-based）或网格引导的策略来实现直观的几何调整。场景修复和创意编辑方法则越来越多地集成2D扩散先验（如Stable Diffusion）来指导3D生成，弥合了2D图像编辑能力与3D空间一致性之间的差距。</span></span></p></section></section></section></section></section></section></section></section></section></section><section style=\"text-align: 团队由国内外知名学者及优秀青年学者组成。本刊被 SCI、Ei、DBLP、INSPEC、SCOPUS 和中国科学引文数据库（CSCD）核心库等收录，为 </span><strong style=\"box-sizing: border-box;\"><span>CCF style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（涵盖 DNA、RNA、蛋白质、配体及其相互作用）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">编码生成式设计任务。Proto 可直接将预测模型整合到生成式工作流中，研究团队利用这一点在人类细胞系中设计并实验验证了可变剪接的内含子。Proto 原生支持多目标优化，能够设计出在合成蛋白-DNA 设计中实验成功率领先的启动子-阻遏物对。结合 AI 智能体，Proto 还可通过自然语言指令实现复杂通路与调控逻辑的定义。</span></span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: mp-quote, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, 设计了能在不同人类细胞系中实现差异化剪接的内含子序列。他们只测试了 65 个候选序列，就有 32% 表现出显著的目标方向差异剪接。相比之下，此前一项类似研究的成功率不到 7%，而且测试了超过 10 万条序列。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span initial;float: none;display: inline !important;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">更令人印象深刻的是，Proto 还能跨模态协同设计——即同时设计 DNA 元件和与之相互作用的蛋白质。研究团队设计了全新的 σ70 启动子</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（称为 ProtoPromoter）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">，其中 86 个候选中有 71 个的活性超过了已知的强启动子 PLtetO1。他们还针对这些启动子设计了对应的阻遏蛋白</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;color: rgb(136, 136, 136);\">（称为 ProtoRepressor）</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\">，成功率达到 46%。最强的设计之一——ProtoRepressor 44_9，对其目标启动子展现出 2 倍的抑制效果，而对非目标启动子几乎没有影响，展现了出色的特异性。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: &#x22;mp-quote&#x22;, initial;float: none;display: inline !important;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">Proto</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;\"> 的开发并非易事。当前的生物学 AI 模型生态“各自为政”，每个工具都有自己的软件依赖、硬件要求和数据格式。Proto 团队为此付出了巨大的工程努力，建立了一套标准化的基础设施，目前已支持超过 120 种工具。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 语言推动了大规模集成电路和计算机程序的发展，Proto 有望成为生物系统设计的“通用语言”。</span></span></p><p style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: normal;\">正如研究团队在论文中所写——随着生物学序列生成式模型、序列到功能预测模型以及基于大语言模型推理的 AI 智能体的不断改进，Proto 所能设计的生物学功能在范围、规模和可靠性方面都将得到提升。随着 Proto 这类生成式生物编程技术的持续成熟，可以设想，</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: normal;font-weight: bold;\">未来的生物学设计将不再受限于天然可用的元件列表，而是受限于人类的创造力。</span></span></p><section style=\"line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 8px;margin-right: 8px;\"><span><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: mp-quote, 编辑丨王多鱼\n\n排版丨水成文\n\n\n\n\n多年来，生物学研究一直依赖于通过试错或筛选成千上万的候选物来组装天然组分，直到找到有效方案。尽管用于蛋白质设计、RNA 工程和基因调控的强大的 AI 模型有望加速这一过程，但这些模型仍各自封闭在计算领域中，许多实验生物学家难以触及。\n\n\n\n\n而现在…,https://www.linkresearcher.com/theses/564861d2-26f8-4508-9c92-c88cb9589e6a,Aditi T. style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">任务一长，Agent 就容易退化成线性的局部试错：试一个方向，失败；换一个方向，再失败；偶尔成功了，也说不清成功背后的机制是什么，更别说把它沉淀下来去指导下一步。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, 63);\">具体来说，系统会给定一个初始</span></span><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\"> artifact——可能是模型训练代码、一个 agent harness，或者一条数据生成 pipeline。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">同时给一个研究目标和一个可执行的 evaluator。Agent 要在没有人逐步盯着的情况下、只能看到 dev 集，通过多轮实验把这个 artifact 一点点改好，最终在 test 集上拿到真实提升。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">这个定义最妙的地方在于：</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;\">它不绑定具体任务。训练模型、改进代码、调整流程</span></span><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;\">……都能被归到 AO 之下。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">它关注的，其实是真实科研里最核心的那个循环</span></span><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">——我们不是让 Agent 回答一道题，而是让它</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;\">持续优化一个研究对象</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, 63);\">这意味着它得长期工作，得处理延迟反馈，得面对失败，还得自己决定下一步往哪走：哪个方向继续、哪个放弃、哪个结果该合并进来。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;\">现有</span></span><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;\"> Agent 到底卡在哪</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">在真实科研里，进展很少来自孤立的一次尝试。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">一个研究者可能同时在想好几个方向：某个看着有潜力，但实验结果不稳；另一个分数涨得明显，可惜疑似只是过拟合了开发集；还有一个虽然失败了，却恰好暴露出关键问题。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">这些信息都得被比较、抽象、保留下来，而不是每一轮都从零开始。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">包含</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">四样东西：</span></span></p><ul class=\"\" style=\"list-style-type: disc;\"><li><p style=\"margin-bottom: 24px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;\">Hypothesis：</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">这个节点想验证的研究主张；</span></span></p></li><li><p style=\"margin-bottom: 24px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;\">Artifact version：</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">该假设对应的代码、配置或数据 pipeline 改动；</span></span></p></li><li><p style=\"margin-bottom: 24px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;\">Distilled insight：</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">这次实验沉淀下来的可复用经验——为什么成、为什么败、在什么条件下有效、哪些方向可能只是局部过拟合、后面该继承还是规避什么。</span></span></p></li></ul><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">每验证完一个假设，</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">Arbor 都会观察结果、提炼出 distilled insight，再沿着父节点把它回写进整棵树，用这一次实验更新对全局的认识。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">所以后续的改进不是在空白上下文里重复试错，而是基于整棵树里已有的假设、证据和经验，动态决定下一步：扩展哪个</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">节点</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino rgb(63, 63, 63);\">、合并哪个改动、剪掉哪个方向，或者生成哪些新的后续假设</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">——尽量让每一步都是当前理解下的最优解。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 24px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">这样一来，这棵假设树就不只是一棵搜索树，也不是普通的实验日志，它同时扮演三个角色：</span></span></p><ul class=\"\" style=\"list-style-type: disc;\"><li><p style=\"margin-bottom: 24px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;\">搜索空间：</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">记录哪些方向在探索、哪些已经失败、哪些值得继续展开；</span></span></p></li><li><p style=\"margin-bottom: 24px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;\">长期记忆：</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">把成功和失败都变成结构化经验，而不是散落在对话历史和日志里；</span></span></p></li><li><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;\">研究记录：</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">把每次</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\"> artifact 改动和它背后的假设、证据、决策连起来，让整个过程可追踪、可审计。</span></span></p></li></ul><section style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span><br></span></section><section style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span><br></span></section><section style=\"text-align: center;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span><img !important;\"><img><img><img><img><img><img><img><img><img><img><img><img><img><img><img><img><img><img><img><img></span></section><p style=\"margin: 32px 0.5em;line-height: 1.75em;text-align: left;font-size: 20px;\"><span style=\"color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;\">把\"长期策略\"和\"短期实验\"分开</span></span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">为了维护这棵树，</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">Arbor 用了一个很干净的两级架构：</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;\">长期存在的 Coordinator 和短期存在的 Executor</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">Coordinator 像\"研究负责人\"：维护全局假设树，观察当前研究状态，提出新假设，挑值得做的方向，再根据结果决定哪些方向继续、剪枝或合并。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">Executor 则像\"实验执行者\"：每个只负责一个具体假设，在隔离的 worktree 里改代码、跑 evaluator、查失败原因，最后把结果结构化地交回去——</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;\">不只有分数，还有 artifact reference、实验现象和 distilled insight。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">这种分工对应着长期科研需要的两种能力：一种是全局策略，知道整个研究走到哪了；一种是局部执行，能把一个具体想法落成代码并跑通。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, 的运行就是一个持续转动的飞轮：</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:
