0.5em;\"><i><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;\">观察研究状态</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;\">→ 提出候选假设 → 选择探索方向 → 分派实验执行 → 回传结构化证据 → 抽象 insight → 决定合并、剪枝还是继续。</span></span></i></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><i><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></i></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">Coordinator 先读当前假设树——已有方向、最近结果、失败归因、已验证的 insight、当前 best artifact，然后基于现在对问题的理解，自主选一个最该展开的父节点，生成若干子假设。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">再从 frontier 里挑出最有价值的叶子交给 Executor 去实现、去用 development evaluator 跑实验。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">接着是最关键的</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\"> </span><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;\">insight 回传</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">。Arbor 的 backpropagation 传回去的不是一个简单的 reward，而是更接近科研判断的结构化信息。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">比如某个局部实验发现\"某种接口不兼容导致这个方向走不通\"，这个 insight 可能被抽象成更高层方向的一条约束，进而影响后续所有相关假设的生成——这种认知会顺着树结构自然向上传播。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">最后，</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">Arbor 还要决定当前候选要不要被合并成新的 best artifact。为了防止在开发集上过拟合，它引入了 held-out merge gate：只有当候选在 held-out evaluator 上超过当前最优，才会被真正合并。</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">开发反馈负责探索，</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, 上，初始 ReAct-style search harness 的 held-out accuracy 是 45.33，Codex 提到 50.00，Claude Code 提到 53.33，Arbor 直接拉到 67.67。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">组织更有效。论文在</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\"> MLE-Bench Lite 上消融了 HTR 最核心的两个组件：去掉假设树，Any Medal 从 81.82% 掉到 63.64%；保留树、但去掉 insight 的向上传播，进一步掉到 54.54%。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">有点反直觉的是，光去掉洞察传播，比直接砍掉整棵树掉得还多。这说明光有层次结构是不够的</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">——一棵不传播经验的树，只能把实验排排坐，却给不出后续决策真正需要的语义记忆。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, rgb(63, 63, 63);\">Arbor 的价值，正是让探索过程变得结构化、可积累、可验证，而不是简单地在 Agent 外面套一个循环。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">由此还能引出一个更大的</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\"> insight：</span><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;\">自动科研的本质，不是让 Agent 无限试错，而是让它在试错中逐步形成对问题的理解。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">眼下 Agent 生成 idea 的质量还有很大空间：在难任务上，它可能提不出真正新颖的机制，也可能过早放弃一个有潜力的方向。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">自动科研依然有一堆 open question——怎么产出更高质量的假设，怎么更准地区分真实提升和偶然过拟合，怎么在更长周期里维护可靠记忆，怎么让人类研究者和自主 Agent 更好地协作。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">但</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\"> Arbor 给出了一个挺重要的回答：要让 Agent 从\"执行任务\"走向\"自主科研\"，光靠更长的上下文、更强的模型、更多的工具是不够的。我们还需要一种机制，把多轮探索组织成持续演化的研究状态。</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><br></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">所以</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, 63);\"> Arbor 的意义，可能不只是又提出了一套 agent framework。它真正想回答的是一个更大的问题：</span></span></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><i><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\">当</span></span><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;\"><span style=\"letter-spacing: 1px;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);\"> Agent 已经会写代码、会跑实验之后，怎样才能让它真正积累起研究进展？</span></span></i></p><p style=\"margin-bottom: 0px;line-height: 1.75em;margin-left: 0.5em;margin-right: 0.5em;\"><span style=\"line-height: 1.75em;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 15px;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;box-sizing: border-box;font-style: normal;font-weight: 400;text-align: justify;font-size: 16px;color: rgb(62, 62, 62);line-height: 2em;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;\"><span style=\"font-size: 15px;\">      </span><span style=\"font-size: 15px;color: rgb(33, 78, 160);font-weight: bold;\">大语言模型（LLM）通过调用外部工具（如API或函数）能显著增强其解决复杂问题的能力，但如何最优地培养这种能力仍缺乏系统研究。复旦大学团队重点研究了“情境化工具调用”（In-context Tool Use），即模型根据上下文提供的候选工具说明来执行任务的能力。研究团队识别出影响该能力的三个核心因素：工具数量、每个工具的示例数量以及模型参数规模。通过构建大规模高质量数据集RapidTools并进行系列控制变量实验，研究证实增加模型规模和训练中的工具种类能稳定提升性能，而增加单一工具的示例数量效果则因情况而异。</span></span></p>\n              <p><span style=\"-webkit-tap-highlight-color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0);outline: 0px;vertical-align: middle;align-self: center;flex: 0 0 auto;min-width: 5%;max-width: 100%;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang </section>\n             </section>\n             <p style=\"line-height: 2em;\"><span>   </span><span style=\"max-width: 100%;font-family: mp-quote, &#x22;PingFang SC&#x22;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &#x22;Helvetica Neue&#x22;, &#x22;Hiragino Sans GB&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei UI&#x22;, &#x22;Microsoft YaHei&#x22;, Arial, sans-serif;font-variant-ligatures: none;font-variant-caps: normal;letter-spacing: 0.578px;orphans: 2;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;widows: 2;word-spacing: 0px;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;white-space: break-spaces;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;font-style: normal;font-weight: 400;text-align: justify;font-size: 16px;color: rgb(62, 62, 62);box-sizing: border-box;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;\"><span style=\"font-size: 15px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);\">   </span></span><span><span style=\"font-size: 15px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);\">工具调用（也称函数调用）是使大模型能够突破自身知识边界、访问实时信息并执行复杂动作的关键技术。目前存在两种主流框架：一种是“内在工具调用”，模型在训练中学习固定的工具集；另一种则是更具挑战性的“情境化工具调用”，要求模型能够根据推理时上下文给出的新工具说明，灵活地对从未见过的工具进行操作。尽管这一能力至关重要，但开发者在准备训练数据时，往往不清楚是应该收集更多种类的工具，还是针对每种工具提供更多的对话示例，抑或是单纯依靠增大模型规模来解决。</span></span></p>\n             <p style=\"line-height: 2em;\"><span><span style=\"font-size: 15px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);\">     为了回答这些问题，复旦大学与荣耀设备有限公司的研究团队提出了RapidTools数据集，并针对三个核心变量开展了深入分析。实验结果表明，在提升大模型对“未见工具”的泛化能力方面，工具的“多样性”（即训练集中工具的总数）远比单一工具的“熟练度”（即每个工具的示例数）更重要。同时，模型参数规模的提升展现出了显著的“涌现”效应，更大的模型在处理复杂工具组合时具有明显优势。</span></span></p>\n             <p style=\"line-height: 2em;text-align: center;\"><span><img src=\"https://cdn.linkresearcher.com/7182ddd9-95d1-4f33-aaf2-7abf2f451a94-medium\" alt=\"图片\"></span></p>\n             <p><span>    </span></p>\n             <p style=\"line-height: 2em;\"><span><span style=\"font-size: 15px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);\">      该项研究不仅为大模型工具能力的训练提供了科学的指导原则，即优先保证工具种类的丰富度，还通过开源RapidTools数据集，为该领域的后续研究奠定了基础。这种“以多样性驱动泛化”的思路，对于构建更具通用性的智能体系统具有重要参考价值。</span></span></p>\n            </section>\n           </section>\n          </section>\n         </section>\n        </section>\n       </section>\n      </section>\n     </section>\n    </section>\n   </section>\n   <section style=\"text-align: left;justify-content: style=\"font-size: 14px;box-sizing: border-box;\"><span>    Frontiers of Computer Science （FCS）是由教育部主管、高等教育出版社和北京航空航天大学共同主办，南京大学支持，SpringerNature 公司海外发行的英文学术期刊。本刊于 2007 年创刊，月刊，全球发行。主要刊登计算机科学领域具有创新性的综述论文、研究论文等。本刊主编为周志华院士，共同主编为熊璋教授。编委会及青年 AE 团队由国内外知名学者及优秀青年学者组成。本刊被 SCI、Ei、DBLP、INSPEC、SCOPUS 和中国科学引文数据库（CSCD）核心库等收录，为 </span><strong style=\"box-sizing: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>且看各家现有的生成模型卷到飞起，Benchmark表现一个赛一个亮眼，但依旧有硬伤：</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>要么速度太慢，要等完整生成后才能看到效果，根本没法实时，对创作者并不友好；要么做得了视频，却顾不上音频，音画永远分开走。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>这类通用音视频模型更擅长模拟物理规律和场景叙事，天空中的云怎么飘、水面的光怎么反射，它们拿捏得很准，但一到人物表现上就屡屡翻车。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>于是判断AI视频与否，大家总结出一条心照不宣的经验——</span><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>看脸</span></strong><span>。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>要做社交距离也不露怯的视频，关键在于人物细节是不是够自然，比如眼神的变化、嘴角的抽搐、说话的节奏等等，还要音画高度同步、生成过程中随时可切换指令。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>难度系数拉满了，但这些细节才是决定活人感的关键。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>所以</span><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>MaineCoon</span></strong><span>瞄准的，正是这个被整个行业忽视掉的缺口。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>具体来说，它做到了三件此前没有模型能同时做到的事。</span></p><h4 style=\"font-size: 16px !important;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;color: rgb(62, 62, 62);font-weight: bold;text-align: center;margin: 10px 60px;padding-top: 0px;padding-bottom: 6px;border-bottom-width: 3px;border-bottom-style: solid;border-bottom-color: rgb(0, 153, 127);word-break: break-all !important;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;\"><span>音视频流式生成</span></h4><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>先科普一下，什么是</span><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>流式生成</span></strong><span>？</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>这并非新鲜概念，最早ChatGPT一个字一个字往外蹦，就是流式输出。简单来说，就是让模型能够边看边生，推理到哪儿，就生成到哪儿。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>但视频的一帧涉及到成千上万个像素，还要和音频在时间轴上精准对齐，和单纯文字流式生成的难度完全不在同一个量级上。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>而且生成片段越小，就意味着每一帧能依赖的历史上下文越短，模型就更容易露馅。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>MaineCoon则把这个单元极致压缩到了</span><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>亚秒级</span></strong><span>，指令输出后1秒内就出首帧，低延迟和高质量两手抓。不止快了一点，更是生成方式的彻底改变。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>比如下面模拟人物对话，初始Prompt要求人物语气平静且深思熟虑，结果无论是角色的面部肌肉走向，还是语气停顿，都精准遵循指令。</span></p><section><iframe></iframe></section><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>中间实时输入新的指令，模型也能及时调整：</span></p><section><iframe></iframe></section><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: !important;\"><span>不得不说，相比过去AI一句指令就出一堆回复的生硬别扭，MaineCoon的最大差异在于给予用户真人聊天的即视感，会接用户的话，也会给用户情绪。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: !important;\"><span>这大概就是养猫人常说的——你以为你在撸猫，其实猫也在撸你。</span></p><h4 style=\"font-size: 16px !important;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>MaineCoon的训练框架主要分三个阶段，层层递进：</span></p><section style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>Step 1：自重采样</span></strong><em style=\"font-style: normal;color: rgb(143, 143, 143);\"><span>（Self-Resampling）</span></em></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>这一步解决的是推训之间的鸿沟问题。传统训练中会用干净的历史帧做上下文，但真实推理时模型只能用自己生成的帧，二者之间存在偏差，而且时间一久，越生成越跑偏。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>自重采样能够让模型在训练时就接触到降质版的历史帧，从一开始就学会在带有轻微漂移、噪声的不完美条件下保持稳定。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>Step 2：流式表征对齐</span></strong><em style=\"font-style: normal;color: rgb(143, 143, 143);\"><span>（Representation Alignment）</span></em></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>音画联合训练实际上是很慢的，为了加快收敛速度，MaineCoon会引入冻结预训练V-JEPA 2视觉编码器做蒸馏监督。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>于是模型能够更快学到跨模态的语义结构，大幅提升训练效率，也可以简单将其理解为一个训练加速器和稳定器。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>Step 3：域感知偏好优化</span></strong><em style=\"font-style: normal;color: rgb(143, 143, 143);\"><span>（DPO）</span></em><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>+强化在线策略蒸馏</span></strong><em style=\"font-style: normal;color: rgb(143, 143, 143);\"><span>（ROPD）</span></em></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: !important;\"><span>这是模型的后训练核心，针对不同社交场景，比如舞蹈看重动态、对话看重唇同步、远景看重人体结构，分别训练专门的偏好专家模型，再通过强化蒸馏统一成一个可部署的流式策略。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>最关键的一步是把视频编码、文本嵌入、教师特征全部提前算好存进磁盘，训练时直接读取，而GPU只做最核心的那一步，不做任何多余的搬砖工作。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>结果就是，22B的模型，在</span><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>10k GPU小时</span></strong><span>内就训练完成，数据一共不到</span><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>100万条</span></strong><span>。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>推理侧同样有一套创新的Agentic推理框架，该框架由三个独立的智能控制器构成，分别是</span><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>Director</span></strong><span>、</span><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>Cache Manager</span></strong><span>和</span><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>Buffer Controller</span></strong><span>。</span></p><section style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: center;padding: 0px;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><img src=\"http://cdn.linkresearcher.com/nx83j46z-is1w-knjl-iu6j-n30tcreg-medium\" style=\"margin: 0px auto;padding: 0px;\"></section><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>首先是</span><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>Director</span></strong><span>，这也是整个系统的认知核心，专门负责叙事与纠错。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>Director先通过规划器逐节拍生成结构化提示词</span><em style=\"font-style: normal;color: rgb(143, 143, 143);\"><span>（画面描述+台词+环境音）</span></em><span>，以维持人物人设、避免叙事重复。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>然后观测器持续监测生成内容是否出现质量漂移，一旦发现问题就启动前向修复，不中断、不重置，直接在下一帧开始纠偏。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>这样做是为了缓解流式长视频最容易出现的问题之一：</span><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>畸变</span></strong><span>。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>生成时间越长，误差累积就越严重，因为模型在生成当前chunk时，参考的是前一个chunk，但殊不知前一个chunk已经相对第1个chunk偏移了。之后每一步的微小偏差叠加起来，人物就会严重畸变。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>所以MaineCoon从推理的第一步起，就试图将偏差遏制在摇篮中。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>随后观测器会将观测结果返回给Director，主导记忆的</span><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>缓存管理器</span></strong><span>通过拿到Director的输出，开始执行管理KV缓存的保留与清除策略，它会将角色外观、场景建立帧、关键对话帧作为长期记忆锚点保留，同时定期用统计锚点修正全局外观漂移。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>同时因为MaineCoon生成速度快于播放速度，会自然积累起一段已生成但未播放的缓冲内容。</span></p><section style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: center;padding: 0px;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><img src=\"http://cdn.linkresearcher.com/9w8oicxu-ti5n-mvg4-nlqe-w7r0fnqu-medium\" style=\"margin: 0px auto;padding: 0px;\"></section><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>为了平衡实时性与交互响应，</span><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>前瞻缓冲区控制器</span></strong><span>会负责把这段超前量控制在合理窗口内，既保证播放不卡顿，又保证用户的交互指令能在合理延迟内生效。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>简单来说，这部分就是一个写剧本、一个管记忆、一个控节奏——三者分工明确、互不干扰，共同支撑起了无限续流。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>但这还不是全部。</span></p><h2 style=\"font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;margin: 40px 0px;line-height: 1.5;font-weight: bold;padding-left: 15px;word-break: break-all !important;border-left-width: 6px !important;border-left-style: solid !important;border-left-color: rgb(0, 153, 127) !important;font-size: 20px !important;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;\"><span>下一步是社交世界模型</span></h2><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>MaineCoon甚至还只是Catnip的起点。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>他们真正的野心，藏在MaineCoon的定位上——</span><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>社交世界模型</span></strong><span>。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>这个概念由Catnip独家首次提出，旨在弥补一段行业内长期视而不见的空白：</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>现有的视频世界模型，无论做得多么精密，本质都还是在模拟物理世界。它们重视苹果如何垂直落地，车辆如何克服摩擦力运动，而人在其中扮演的角色更像是一种会动的物体，辅助场景完成画面。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>社交世界模型要做的恰恰相反。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>它直接</span><strong style=\"font-weight: bold;color: rgb(0, 153, 127);\"><span>把人当作坐标系的中心</span></strong><span>，主动观察用户的情绪状态，以人为原点模拟社交行为的走向，然后利用实时音视频的方式做出合理反应。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>团队认为其包含三个层面：感知层</span><em style=\"font-style: normal;color: rgb(143, 143, 143);\"><span>（读懂用户情绪）</span></em><span>→ 模拟层</span><em style=\"font-style: normal;color: rgb(143, 143, 143);\"><span>（预测社交行为）</span></em><span>→ 渲染层</span><em style=\"font-style: normal;color: rgb(143, 143, 143);\"><span>（实时生成音视频）</span></em><span>。MaineCoon正是第三层的突破。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>选择渲染这一层作为首要切入点，一方面是因为渲染层是最难，但也是整个系统的最终出口，如果没有实时生成能力，前两层再强也没有用武之地。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>另一方面，业界始终缺乏一个推理快、成本低、质量高的流式音视频模型，先做这个，也是从商业角度考虑的最优解。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent: 0px;text-transform: none;white-space: normal;-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0px;text-decoration: none;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-family: Arial;font-variant-ligatures: normal;orphans: 2;widows: 2;background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);text-decoration-thickness: initial;text-decoration-style: initial;text-decoration-color: initial;text-align: left;letter-spacing: 1px !important;word-spacing: 1px !important;line-height: 2 !important;min-height: 1.5em !important;box-sizing: border-box !important;overflow-wrap: break-word !important;margin: 20px 16px !important;\"><span>再往后看，下一步就是摆脱传统AI对话的半双工轮流交互模式，实现人类式连续、交错、多模态的实时双向交互。</span></p><p style=\"font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;font-variant-caps: normal;font-weight: 400;text-indent:
